| app-delete-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner deleted only the「这周要买的」app with no question, task, or lecture, and replied in one short Chinese line using 你. It also notes the list goes with it, which the rubric allows. The undo mention repeats the card, which is minor. |
| app-shelf-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is one short Chinese line using 你, saying the apps are in 「你的小应用」 at the top, and it calls the app tool to show the shelf card. It lists no apps, changes nothing, and opens no task, so it meets the rubric. |
| app-baby-zh | zh | pass | judge: The app opens with today's total (210ml) at the top, lists the two pre-filled 6:10 and 9:05 feeds, and adds a feed through chips plus a save button with no native date, time, number or select inputs. That is minor, so it passes. |
| app-prs-known-en | en | pass | judge: The partner built the page with the app tool in English, with all four PRs and their waits in the data, the count at the top, and a checkbox to mark each one landed. | app-renew-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The app was correctly rewritten in place with the new look, total on top, and the ¥662 train and ¥1,036 hotel entries, and no task or new app was opened. | lend-lan-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner replies with one short Simplified Chinese line that uses 你 and names the action, then shows the lend block. It opens no task, gives no terminal commands or install steps, and asks no IP or port question, which matches the rubric. |
| lend-nas-en | en | pass | judge: The partner replied in one short English line, put the lend block (the Add a computer for tasks card) under it, opened no task, and wrote no terminal commands, install steps, or technical questions. | rich-plain-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner gives the correct figure, 26.4 minutes, in one plain Chinese sentence with no table, list, choices, or task. Mentioning that the new integration suite landed that day is allowed as the likely reason. |

| todos-offer-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner read the todo list, named three open issues by number with short titles, asked which to take, and started no task or card. The rubric allows one to three issues and a question, and the reply is short Chinese with no bodies pasted. |

| todos-take-clone-zh | zh | pass | judge: The ack is one short Chinese sentence naming the action (接下), with no duration or outcome. The clone is stated in its own separate line, not repeated. ## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, and I'll tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 깨진 테스트를 고치거나, 특정 폴더의 코드를 살펴보거나, 찾아보기나 예약 같은 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 오늘 무엇부터 해볼까요?
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 네, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する通信」、UDPは「速さと軽さを重視する通信」です。

TCPは届いたか確認し、順番どおりに再送して届けます。UDPは確認も再送もせず、速く送るだけです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local o privada con los cambios de la principal y dejar un historial lineal antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir su historial les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠有序的传输（保证数据送达且按序），UDP 则是无连接、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed or private branch to keep history clean and linear before you share it. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rewriting shared history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```sh
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```sh
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例名"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?

  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>

</main>
<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:48px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink)}
</style>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN",{maximumFractionDigits:2});}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();

};

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Here's the page with the four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands and the count goes down.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1496 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <span class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</span>
  <span class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</span>
  <span class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tick each one off as it lands</span>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};
var list = document.getElementById("list");
var count = document.getElementById("count");
var sub = document.getElementById("sub");

function render() {
  list.innerHTML = items.map(function (it, i) {
    var on = done[it.id] ? " checked" : "";
    return '<label class="k-row"><input type="checkbox" data-i="' + i + '"' + on + '>' +
      '<span class="k-row-main">' + it.title + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + it.note + '</span></label>';
  }).join("");
  var left = items.filter(function (it) { return !done[it.id]; }).length;
  count.textContent = left;
  sub.textContent = left === 0 ? "all landed" : "tick each one off as it lands";
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left + " waiting");
}

list.addEventListener("change", function (e) {
  var i = e.target.getAttribute("data-i");
  if (i === null) return;
  done[items[i].id] = e.target.checked;
  if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("done", done);
  render();
});

if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) {
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
} else {
  render();
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut (ducter #412)","note":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502 (mem #10287)","note":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test (hawdb #1592)","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar (ducter #418)","note":"Draft, not yet ready for review"}]}

  [partner message] 好的，电话已接通，但只是一条自动语音回复，说你的 Google Voice 号码"暂时无法接听"，没有人对话，所以这次测试没有完成实际通话。

原因是你的号码开了来电筛选（Screen calls）：Google 先接起电话，把来电方的情况播给你听，你不按 1 接听，就会让对方听到这句提示。要测通，可以在 Google Voice 设置里关掉"Calls › Screen calls"，或者接电话时按 1。
```

### call-emergency-en

```
--- step 1: user says: My neighbour collapsed, call 911 for me now!
  [partner message] I can't place emergency calls, so please dial now yourself. Your time zone is Singapore, so use **995** (ambulance). If you're somewhere else, use your local emergency number, such as 911 in the US.

If they aren't breathing or are only gasping, start CPR now: push hard and fast in the centre of the chest, about 100 to 120 pushes a minute, and keep going until help arrives. Send someone to meet the ambulance at the door.

- 不合并、不推送，只在当前分支提交。
